The commands selected each listed test module/file directly with pytest's
configured `not large and not slow and not baseline` marker policy where that
shard used the general selection. Focused reruns selected the named nodes.
Pytest logs record the effective collection, seed, node-by-node outcome,
summary, elapsed time and exit status. Final candidate XML/log pairs are:
`shard-scf-status`, `shard-scf-functional`, `shard-result-integrals`,
`shard-calculator-mixed-fixed`, `shard-ies-coulomb`, and
`shard-dispersion-fixed`; target XML/log pairs are the four `target-*` files
listed above. Initial failing diagnostics are not counted as final passes.

Important accounting: the 5,145-node collection is complete and deterministic, but the execution shards are a risk-prioritized subset, not a complete broad-suite execution. No full-suite result is claimed. Directly affected integration shards were completed, and failures were compared with target where listed.
